# Declined, limited and pending rules

Generated by `python scripts/declined_rules.py` from the coverage inventories and the curated research decisions in that script. Every entry is a rule candidate that Render Lens does not implement (or implements only partially) and the reason. Pending items are not counted as rules.

| Category | Entries |
| --- | --- |
| Godot does not expose the data | 159 |
| Unreachable: the engine prevents the condition | 5 |
| Environment-bound (editor, bake/import, platform, build, device or runtime) | 324 |
| Runtime script state, not stored in scenes, resources or settings | 353 |
| Would flag Godot's defaults, is not a configuration problem, or has no reliable cost direction | 74 |
| Already handled by Godot or by another rule | 102 |
| Asset or scene data that Godot repairs, converts or skips on load and reports itself | 97 |
| Engine internals, leak reports and developer diagnostics | 40 |
| Implemented with a stated gap | 38 |
| Pending research (not counted as rules) | 0 |
| Removed by product policy | 5 |
| Keyword matches that are not costs (class reference and tutorials) | 432 |

## Godot does not expose the data

| Candidate | Reason | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Barriers, descriptor updates, redundant binds, allocation flags, transient/lazy memory, queue ownership, pipeline-cache handles, render-pass load/store | Require RenderingDevice-internal command state that the plugin cannot read. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (Excluded checks) |
| BestPractices-AMD-ClearAttachment-ClearImage-color (engine state unavailable) | Requires actual standalone clear/resolve/copy command selection and, for copies, differing source/destination tilings. MSAA, texture updates and viewport settings do not determine the native command path. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_copy_blit_resolve.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-ClearAttachment-ClearImage-depth-stencil (engine state unavailable) | Requires actual standalone clear/resolve/copy command selection and, for copies, differing source/destination tilings. MSAA, texture updates and viewport settings do not determine the native command path. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_copy_blit_resolve.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-ClearAttachment-FastClearValues-color (engine state unavailable) | Requires actual vkCmdClearAttachments clear values and attachment aspects. The depth/stencil predicate combines non-endpoint depth with nonzero stencil. Godot background colors are not evidence that this command or attachment path was used. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_copy_blit_resolve.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-ClearAttachment-FastClearValues-depth-stencil (engine state unavailable) | Requires actual vkCmdClearAttachments clear values and attachment aspects. The depth/stencil predicate combines non-endpoint depth with nonzero stencil. Godot background colors are not evidence that this command or attachment path was used. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_copy_blit_resolve.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-CmdBuffer-highBarrierCount (engine state unavailable) | Counts image/buffer barriers submitted during the frame. Godot draw-call and GPU-pass counts do not determine that native command count. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_synchronization.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-CreatePipelines-AvoidPrimitiveRestart (not observable from ordinary scene meshes) | Both checked Vulkan drivers enable restart only for RenderingDevice triangle strips WITH_RESTART_INDEX. Mesh triangle-strip topology alone cannot identify that mode. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_pipeline.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-CreatePipelines-MinimizeNumDynamicStates (engine state unavailable) | Requires native dynamic-state list length or live pipeline-object count. Material/node counts and Godot compilation counters do not equal those quantities. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_pipeline.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-CreatePipelines-TooManyPipelines (engine state unavailable) | Requires native dynamic-state list length or live pipeline-object count. Material/node counts and Godot compilation counters do not equal those quantities. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_pipeline.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-UpdateDescriptors-AvoidCopyingDescriptors (engine state unavailable) | Requires native descriptor copy/update-template calls. Godot resource/scene data does not reveal which update API path was submitted. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_descriptor.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-UpdateDescriptors-PreferNonTemplate (engine state unavailable) | Requires native descriptor copy/update-template calls. Godot resource/scene data does not reveal which update API path was submitted. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_descriptor.cpp |
| BestPractices-AMD-VkCommandBuffer-AvoidSecondaryCmdBuffers (disabled in checked stock Godot builds) | Godot 4.4 and 4.7.2 rendering_device.cpp both define SECONDARY_COMMAND_BUFFERS_PER_FRAME as zero. | Button outline plus shadow passes | Button has an outline pass but no font-shadow pass in either native implementation, so the two-pass rule cannot apply. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.19) |
| Cooperative-matrix reservation and subgroup feature/flag/size state | Not public audit evidence in Godot's RenderingDevice API. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.21) |
| Depth attachment formats and index-cache efficiency | Not inferable from scene settings; emulating a GPU vertex cache would need expensive mesh readbacks and still not know the submitted draw range. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (Excluded checks) |
| Executed descriptor indices, live addresses, heap mappings, binding ranges, inter-invocation races (GPU-assisted validation) | Ordinary scene data and imported shader declarations do not expose them. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.12) |
| Godot 4.4 Vulkan limits MAX_COMPUTE_SHARED_MEMORY_SIZE and MetalFX scaler min/max | 4.4's Vulkan limit_get logs an error instead of returning these; metrics skip them before 4.7 and shared-memory rules are 4.7-only. | drivers/vulkan/rendering_device_driver_vulkan.cpp (4.4) |
| Line2D antialiased batching note | The antialiased property is stored but not passed to Line2D's triangle draw path in 4.4 or 4.7.2, so the documented batching effect cannot be observed. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.8) |
| Node configuration warnings via get_configuration_warnings() | Not bound to scripts; every native predicate is reimplemented and tested instead. Script _get_configuration_warnings overrides are never executed. | scene/main/node.cpp; docs/GODOT_COVERAGE.json |
| PhysicalSkyMaterial quarter-resolution sky | No public quarter-resolution property in the checked tags. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.8) |
| Ray-query support on Godot 4.4 | 4.4 does not expose SUPPORTS_RAY_QUERY; absence is treated as unknown, and ray-query rules are 4.7-gated. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.9) |
| Remaining cooperative-vector checks in ValidateCooperativeVector (VUIDs 10089, 10091, 10094, 10095) | Every static clause of the function is implemented (10090, 10092, 10093 and the spec's 10096-10101, 11809); the remaining four compare against cooperativeVectorProperties, supported stages and maxCooperativeVectorComponents, which Godot does not expose. | Vulkan-ValidationLayers cc_spirv_cooperative.cpp (pinned 99fd2cb5) |
| RichTextLabel outline/shadow passes (extension of ui.label_effect_passes) | Script-pushed formatting can override spans and the private stack_externally_modified state is not exposed. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.19) |
| Vulkan handle, layout and synchronization validity | Engine-internal API usage; not observable from a plugin. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (Excluded checks) |
| layers/core_checks/cc_device_memory.cpp: HasTileMemoryType and ValidateTileMemoryBindInfo only (engine state unavailable) | HasTileMemoryType resolves allocation memoryTypeIndex to heapIndex and tests VK_MEMORY_HEAP_TILE_MEMORY_BIT_QCOM. ValidateTileMemoryBindInfo requires the supplied native allocation to come from such a heap (VUID-VkTileMemoryBindInfoQCOM-memory-10726). The audit cannot recover the native allocation/heap/command binding association. Other allocation, map, bind, device-group and memory-budget validators in this file remain unreviewed. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/core_checks/cc_drawdispatch.cpp: ValidateBoundTileMemory only (engine state unavailable) | Compares each bound resource memory allocation from a tile-memory heap against the command buffer active tile-memory handle (action VUID 10746). Requires native VkDeviceMemory binding identities, memory heap flags and command-buffer state. Godot scene nodes, imported shader declarations and public device limits do not provide those associations. No warning can be inferred from ordinary Godot tile maps, texture memory totals or renderer selection. Other draw/dispatch validators in this file remain unreviewed. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/core_checks/cc_pipeline_compute.cpp: Complete compute-pipeline entry dispatch and ValidateComputePipelineDerivatives; delegated shader/layout validators are separate scopes (native pipeline state unavailable) | Direct checks require native derivative/base pipeline handles and creation flags, creation-array ordering, independent-set layout flags and embedded sampler counts. These are not observable from a Godot scene or imported shader declaration. The file also delegates shader, robustness and SPIR-V checks elsewhere; reading this dispatcher does not claim those validators are exhausted. No generic compute-shader performance warning is warranted. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/buffer_device_address.cpp: Unallocated-reference and Aligned-operand error handlers (runtime state unavailable) | Requires executed load/store addresses, byte widths and live committed address ranges. Existing capability checks do not prove bounds or alignment. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/descriptor_checks_buffer.cpp: Missing and wrong-type descriptor hash handlers (runtime state unavailable) | Requires executed descriptor-buffer locations and hashes of actual encoded native descriptors, not resource declarations. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/descriptor_checks_classic.cpp: Array bounds, uninitialized/destroyed descriptors, buffer/cooperative-matrix/texel bounds (runtime state unavailable) | Requires actual accessed indices and descriptor allocation/update/lifetime records, bound ranges and resource robustness state; scene material counts and declared array lengths alone are insufficient. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/descriptor_checks_heap.cpp: Heap bounds/reserved ranges, descriptor/push/address alignment, null addresses and descriptor hashes (runtime state unavailable) | Requires native heap mappings, push-data snapshots, addresses, alignments and encoded descriptor contents. Static set numbers are not heap offsets. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/mesh_shading.cpp: SetMeshOutputs and task-payload error handlers (unsupported public shader stage) | Checked Godot public RDShader stages expose no mesh/task stage. GPUAV compares executed output counts and actual pipeline task-stage presence. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/post_process_descriptor_indexing.cpp: Submission completion routing into DescriptorValidator::ValidateBindingDynamic (runtime state unavailable) | Uses GPU-recorded descriptor accesses plus current native descriptor bindings, shader identities and command locations. The downstream descriptor validator needs separate source review. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/shared_memory_data_race.cpp: Store/load/atomic race error handlers (runtime state unavailable) | Requires overlapping accesses from executed invocations, shadow-memory state and instruction locations. Shared-memory declarations or absence of a barrier do not establish a race. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/vertex_attribute_fetch_oob.cpp: GetVertexAttributeFetchLimits and indexed vertex/instance access handlers (runtime state unavailable) | Requires active pipeline input locations, bound vertex buffer sizes/strides/offsets, instance divisors and executed indices. | layers/gpuav/spirv/mesh_shading_pass.cpp: Mesh-stage gate, execution-mode output limits, SetMeshOutputs and task-payload checks (unsupported public shader stage) | The path requires a mesh-stage shader and task/mesh pipeline state. Neither checked Godot RenderingDevice public ShaderStage enum exposes mesh/task stages. Do not equate Vulkan mesh-shader output counts with MeshInstance3D/MultiMesh node counts. The upstream safe-mode clamp is an instrumentation safeguard, not a project edit recommendation. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/vertex_attribute_fetch_oob_pass.cpp: Instrument: vertex-stage/input gate, entry-point insertion and stage-information payload (runtime state unavailable) | The pass injects a call using executed vertex/instance stage information and the live draw/binding data traced in the existing vertex-attribute handler review. | layers/gpuav/validation_cmd/gpuav_dispatch.cpp: Indirect dispatch count validation (runtime state unavailable) | Actual indirect buffer counts are unavailable; workgroup local size is not dispatch group count. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |

## Unreachable: the engine prevents the condition

| Candidate | Reason | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| LineEdit secret character longer than one character | set_secret_character truncates to one character (with WARN_PRINT) in 4.4 and 4.7.2, so the warning cannot fire from stored state. | godot/scene/gui/line_edit.cpp; docs/GODOT_COVERAGE.json |
| LineEdit warning: Secret Character property supports only one character. Extra characters will be ignored. | Unreachable from stored state: set_secret_character truncates to one character in 4.4 and 4.7.2. | docs/GODOT_COVERAGE.json |
| SubViewport smaller than 2 px (Viewport::get_configuration_warnings) | Viewport::_set_size clamps both axes with maxi(2) in 4.4 and 4.7.2, so stored state cannot produce the warning; the positive fixture read back (2, 64). | godot/scene/main/viewport.cpp; docs/GODOT_COVERAGE.json |
| Viewport warning: The Viewport size must be greater than or equal to 2 pixels on both dimensions to render anything. | Unreachable from stored state: Viewport::_set_size clamps both axes to at least 2 pixels in 4.4 and 4.7.2. | docs/GODOT_COVERAGE.json |
| tutorials/performance/vertex_animation/animating_thousands_of_fish: For both color_format and custom_data_format you can choose... (tutorial paragraph) | Describes Godot 3 MultiMesh properties (color_format, custom_data_format); the 4.4 and 4.7 MultiMesh only has the use_colors and use_custom_data booleans, so there is no format to check. | docs/GODOT_TUTORIAL_NOTES.json |

## Would flag Godot's defaults, is not a configuration problem, or has no reliable cost direction

| Candidate | Reason | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| AnimationMixer deterministic mode | Trades normalization work against reset writes; without active track weights there is no reliable cost direction. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.8) |
| AudioServer.PLAYBACK_TYPE_SAMPLE (class-reference constant note) | Web-only playback type whose sample mode is already the default there. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| AudioStreamGenerator.buffer_length (class-reference member note) | Procedural audio buffer sized together with the script that fills it; the latency/CPU trade-off has no default to compare against. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| AudioStreamGenerator.mix_rate (class-reference member note) | Procedural audio sample rate chosen with the script that generates it; no default violation to report. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| BaseMaterial3D.SPECULAR_DISABLED (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| CSGShape3D.smoothing_angle (class-reference member note) | Disabling smoothing is a visual choice with a minor one-time CSG rebuild saving. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ColorPicker.deferred_mode (class-reference member note) | The cost depends on scripts connected to color_changed; immediate mode is the default. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| CurveTexture.width (class-reference member note) | One-time generation of at most 4096 texels; negligible memory. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| CurveXYZTexture.width (class-reference member note) | One-time generation of at most 4096 texels; negligible memory. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| DirectionalLight2D.max_distance (class-reference member note) | Requires comparing shadow casters with the camera's visible extent; the default (10000 px) is not a problem by itself. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| DirectionalLight3D.SHADOW_PARALLEL_2_SPLITS (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Environment.AMBIENT_SOURCE_COLOR (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Environment.AMBIENT_SOURCE_DISABLED (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Environment.REFLECTION_SOURCE_DISABLED (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Environment.TONE_MAPPER_ACES (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Environment.TONE_MAPPER_FILMIC (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Environment.TONE_MAPPER_REINHARDT (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Generic TileMap quadrant-size advice | Reducing quadrant size alone does not establish wasted work; replaced by tilemap_small_quadrants, which requires populated-cell evidence. | docs/UPSTREAM_RULES.md (1.8) |
| GraphEdit warning: Please be aware that GraphEdit and GraphNode will undergo extensive refactoring in a future 4.x version involv | API-stability notice for GraphEdit/GraphNode, not a configuration problem. | docs/GODOT_COVERAGE.json |
| HTTPRequest.download_chunk_size (class-reference member note) | Network throughput/memory trade-off; no frame cost is observable from configuration. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| HTTPRequest.use_threads (class-reference member note) | Network throughput option; main-thread polling cost is not measurable from configuration. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| IK/spring-bone mutable_bone_axes | Defaults to true; the documented gain from disabling it is 'slight' and only safe when bone pose positions never change. | doc/classes/IKModifier3D.xml, SpringBoneSimulator3D.xml |
| IKModifier3D.mutable_bone_axes (class-reference member note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Light2D.SHADOW_FILTER_PCF5 (class-reference constant note) | The cheaper soft-shadow filter; PCF13 is flagged by canvas.pcf13. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Light3D.BAKE_DYNAMIC (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Mesh.create_convex_shape (class-reference method note) | One-time shape generation speed; shapes saved in scenes are analysed by the physics rules. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| MeshInstance3D.create_convex_collision (class-reference method note) | One-time shape generation speed; shapes saved in scenes are analysed by the physics rules. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| OmniLight3D.SHADOW_CUBE (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| OmniLight3D.SHADOW_DUAL_PARABOLOID (class-reference constant note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ProjectSettings.audio/general/default_playback_type.web (class-reference member note) | Web-only playback mode whose default (Sample) is already the performance choice. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ProjectSettings.input_devices/buffering/agile_event_flushing (class-reference member note) | An Android input-responsiveness option; the note states no cost to detect. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ProjectSettings.rendering/2d/batching/uniform_set_cache_size (class-reference member note) | Needs the number of unique textures drawn per frame, which Godot does not expose. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ProjectSettings.rendering/rendering_device/vulkan/max_descriptors_per_pool (class-reference member note) | Driver-level descriptor pool tuning with no symptom observable from the project. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ProjectSettings.xr/openxr/foveation_dynamic (class-reference member note) | Adaptive foveation behaviour depends on the XR runtime and frame rate at play time. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| RenderingServer.set_debug_generate_wireframes (class-reference method note) | A debug toggle that is off by default; a game turns it on deliberately for a wireframe view. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceImporterCSVTranslation.compress (class-reference member note) | Smaller files for a small CPU overhead; the Auto default is the documented recommendation. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceImporterLayeredTexture.compress/channel_pack (class-reference member note) | Channel layout choice for sRGB correctness; the memory difference is format-specific and not flagged. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceImporterScene.animation/fps (class-reference member note) | Animation sampling rate trades memory for motion accuracy; no default violation or measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceImporterScene.animation/remove_immutable_tracks (class-reference member note) | Animation memory saving whose effect depends on track contents; not measurable from the import settings alone. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceImporterScene.animation/trimming (class-reference member note) | Animation memory saving whose effect depends on keyframe contents; not measurable from the import settings alone. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceImporterTexture.compress/channel_pack (class-reference member note) | Channel layout choice for sRGB correctness; the memory difference is format-specific and not flagged. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceImporterTexture.detect_3d/compress_to (class-reference member note) | Controls automatic reimport when a texture is detected in 3D; the resulting compress/mode is what the import rules check. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceImporterTextureAtlas.import_mode (class-reference member note) | Region versus mesh atlases trade vertices for overdraw; deciding needs an overdraw capture, not import settings. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| ResourceLoader.load_threaded_request (class-reference method note) | A per-call trade-off (use_sub_threads: faster loads, possible main-thread slowdowns) with no project-level setting to compare. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Shape3D.margin (class-reference member note) | Engine-specific collision tuning (unused by Godot Physics); the default margin is the recommended value. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| SpringBoneSimulator3D.mutable_bone_axes (class-reference member note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| SurfaceTool.index (class-reference method note) | Procedural mesh-building API used by scripts; imported meshes are always indexed. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| SurfaceTool.optimize_indices_for_cache (class-reference method note) | Procedural mesh-building API used by scripts; imported meshes are always indexed. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| TileMapLayer._update_cells (class-reference method note) | Implementing the virtual is a deliberate choice and the warning states no threshold to compare against. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| TileSetAtlasSource.use_texture_padding (class-reference member note) | Padding is the default protection against tile-seam artifacts; the note calls disabling it a small gain. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| VisualShaderNodeDerivativeFunc.PRECISION_FINE (class-reference constant note) | An explicit precision choice (dFdxFine/dFdyFine) made when coarse derivatives are not precise enough; the note gives no cost size and the default precision is neither. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| VoxelGI two bounces (VoxelGIData.use_two_bounces) | Defaults to true in 4.4 and 4.7.2; a rule would fire on every baked VoxelGI. Dropped after the negative fixture exposed the default. | doc/classes/VoxelGIData.xml; docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| VoxelGIData.use_two_bounces (class-reference member note) | The note describes Godot's default trade-off; flagging every default use would add noise without a measured symptom. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Window.CONTENT_SCALE_MODE_CANVAS_ITEMS (class-reference constant note) | Godot recommends canvas_items stretch for most non-pixel-art games; the cost buys resolution-independent 2D. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |

## Already handled by Godot or by another rule

| Candidate | Reason | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2D MSAA enabled without 2D content | Godot prints "2D MSAA is enabled while there is no 2D content. Disable 2D MSAA for better performance." once at runtime; a static scan cannot see 2D content added by other scenes or autoloads. | servers/rendering/renderer_viewport.cpp:756 (4.7.2) |
| BestPractices-Arm-vkBeginCommandBuffer-one-time-submit (already followed by checked stock Godot) | Both checked Godot Vulkan drivers set VK_COMMAND_BUFFER_USAGE_ONE_TIME_SUBMIT_BIT in primary and secondary command-buffer begin info (4.4 lines 2825/2844; 4.7.2 lines 3466/3487). No project setting is needed. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_cmd_buffer.cpp |
| BestPractices-Arm-vkCreateSwapchainKHR-swapchain-presentmode-not-fifo (related Godot guidance already exists) | The exact native present mode can differ from the requested Godot VSync setting. Existing pacing checks already offer caps/VSync and explain mailbox tradeoffs; duplicating them as a proven Arm FIFO violation would overstate the evidence. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_wsi.cpp |
| BestPractices-NVIDIA-vkBeginCommandBuffer-one-time-submit (already followed by checked stock Godot) | Both checked Godot Vulkan drivers set VK_COMMAND_BUFFER_USAGE_ONE_TIME_SUBMIT_BIT in primary and secondary command-buffer begin info (4.4 lines 2825/2844; 4.7.2 lines 3466/3487). No project setting is needed. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_cmd_buffer.cpp |
| BestPractices-vkCmdEndRenderPass-depth-pre-pass-usage (related Godot guidance already exists) | Requires observed depth-only/equal-depth draw patterns and attachment usage within a native render pass. Existing Godot prepass/vendor-exception guidance covers the project setting without claiming the layer predicate fired. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json layers/best_practices/bp_render_pass.cpp |
| CSGShape3D.bake_collision_shape (class-reference method note) | The shape is baked at runtime by a script; concave shapes saved in scenes are covered by physics.concave_shape. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| Environment.set_glow_level (class-reference method note) | Each enabled level adds one blur pass at a quarter of the previous level's area (glow loop in renderer_scene_render_rd.cpp), so levels 6-7 add a small fraction of the glow cost that post.glow_enabled already reports. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| FastNoiseLite.TYPE_SIMPLEX_SMOOTH (class-reference constant note) | One-time noise generation; the size and octave cost drivers are covered by scene_validation noise checks. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| FastNoiseLite.TYPE_VALUE_CUBIC (class-reference constant note) | One-time noise generation; the size and octave cost drivers are covered by scene_validation noise checks. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |
| NoiseTexture2D.generate_mipmaps (class-reference member note) | Standard mipmap memory (about a third more), shared by every mipmapped texture; noise size and octave costs are covered by scene_validation noise checks. | docs/GODOT_PERF_NOTES.json |

## Implemented with a stated gap

| Candidate | Reason | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Cooperative-matrix MulAdd contracts (scopes, M/N/K, operand uses, signedness flags) | Implemented as scene_validation.shader_matrix_muladd for known cooperative-matrix value types and 32-bit constant dimensions; specialization-constant dimensions and values from untracked instructions are skipped. | SPIRV-Tools validate_arithmetics.cpp ValidateCooperativeMatrixMulAddKHR (main, read 2026-10-12) |
| Cooperative-vector offset/stride alignment (VUID-RuntimeSpirv 10096-10101) and 32-bit offsets (11809) | Implemented as scene_validation.shader_cooperative_alignment and shader_cooperative_offset_width for constant offsets/strides and known offset types in the first entry block. Buffer base-pointer alignment, dynamic and specialized offsets cannot be known statically. | Vulkan spec spirvenv (read 2026-10-12) |
| Empty child CSG brushes under a non-empty root | CSGShape3D only exposes the root result through get_meshes(); child brushes are private. csg_empty_shape covers an empty root (and reports its children). | modules/csg/csg_shape.cpp |
| Label missing glyphs in translated text | label_missing_glyphs shapes the untranslated source text (uppercase applied, up to 4096 characters); Label's private translated/shaped paragraphs are not exposed. | scene/gui/label.cpp |
| SpringBoneSimulator3D unsorted/duplicate exclusion paths | Reproduced on Godot 4.4 and 4.7.2 (an excluded collision still pushes the bones) and implemented as scene_validation.spring_bone_exclude_order; not reported upstream yet. | scene/3d/spring_bone_simulator_3d.cpp _find_collisions; tests/test_source_rules.gd (2026-10-12) |
| Subgroup-uniform-control-flow conflict beyond the four repack opcodes | scene_validation.shader_subgroup_repack now follows the Vulkan spec's ray-tracing-repack list (13 opcodes, including the NV/EXT shader-execution-reorder instructions); current Vulkan-ValidationLayers still checks four. Fixtures are synthesized bytecode; no Godot compiler emits the reorder instructions without the extension. | Vulkan spec raytracing.adoc ray-tracing-repack; SPIR-V grammar (read 2026-10-12) |
| layers/core_checks/cc_descriptor.cpp: GetDescriptorCountMaxPerStage; PreCallValidateCreatePipelineLayout descriptor counts; uniform-buffer range checks (partially adapted) | Known fixed compute declarations and public Vulkan limits establish lower bounds. Runtime descriptor writes, dynamic offset use and the rest of this file remain outside this observation model. Since 1.16, applicable resource/operand checks inspect each public imported shader stage independently, including Godot 4.7 ray stages; workgroup checks remain compute-only. Stage quantities are not summed across unverified pipelines. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/core_checks/cc_image.cpp: Complete bodies of ValidateClearDepthStencilValue and ValidateImageViewFormatFeatures only; other image validators remain pending (partially adapted; unavailable clear/view state documented) | Clear depth must lie in [0,1] unless the native depth-range-unrestricted extension is enabled, also rejecting NaN. Historical clear arguments and enabled extension state are not observable from scene camera/viewport settings. The reviewed image-view storage-format feature requirement is adapted to explicit imported image formats and the current public Vulkan device query: both optimal and linear tiling must lack storage support. Actual view usage, DRM modifier/external image features, other extension-dependent branches and command execution are not inferred. Image creation, view/copy/clear ranges and validators outside the two named bodies remain pending. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/core_checks/cc_image_layout.cpp: ValidateDescriptorImageLayout, ValidateSubresourceImageLayout and ValidateClearImageLayout only (partial core image-layout review) | These checks compare an explicit descriptor/copy/clear layout with per-command-buffer layout tracking for the exact image aspect, layer and mip range. Standalone clear operations also constrain their VkImageLayout argument. Godot owns these layouts internally; neither checked public RenderingDevice API exposes the executed command-buffer layout map. A texture format, material or viewport clear mode cannot establish a layout mismatch. Other render-pass, barrier, submission and host-copy validators in this file remain separate review scopes. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/core_checks/cc_shader_interface.cpp: ValidateBuiltInLimits, ValidatePrimitiveTopology, ValidatePipelineTessellationStages and ValidateInterfaceBetweenStages, ValidateInterfaceVertexInput, ValidateInterfaceFragmentOutput, ValidateShaderInputAttachmentDynamicRendering, ValidateShaderInputAttachmentRenderPass and ValidateFsOutputsAgainstRenderPass, ValidateDrawDynamicRenderingFsOutputs, ValidateDrawRenderingTileMemoryOutputs and ValidateGraphicsPipelineShaderState (all function bodies in this file; delegated validators retain their own scopes) (partial static adaptation) | Vertex/fragment pairs with no extra stages now check incompatible numeric kinds/widths and proven missing producer slots. Missing-output findings require complete supported vertex reflection; bounded/unknown declarations suppress them. Signedness is ignored. Unused outputs and larger producer vectors depend on maintenance4 and are not inferred. Built-in and tessellation interface matching remain outside this adaptation. Primitive-topology checks require an unexposed geometry stage. Previously reviewed clip/cull/sample-mask limits remain unavailable, and four invalid/missing tessellation layout modes already produce compiler errors on both Godot versions. Additional 1.18 review: vertex-input type/width/missing/unused checks require actual vertex attribute formats and enabled vertexAttributeRobustness/maintenance9. Fragment alpha-to-coverage requires its pipeline state. Input attachment checks require the associated subpass or dynamic-rendering count/remapping. Fragment output warnings require bound attachment formats, color-write masks and alpha-to-coverage state. Public RenderingDevice creation APIs accept these values but do not enumerate live shader/pipeline associations or recover all required creation state. An imported RDShaderFile alone is insufficient; no rule added from these five functions. The dynamic-rendering draw checks also require bound image views, location remapping and effective color-write/alpha-to-coverage/rasterization state. Tile-memory output delegates ValidateBoundTileMemory using command-buffer active attachments; the delegated predicate is recorded separately under cc_drawdispatch.cpp. The graphics-pipeline dispatcher orders real linked stages and delegates per-stage validation, interface validation and tessellation checks. This completes the function-body review of this file, not every delegated validator or all VVL research. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/core_checks/cc_spirv.cpp: Complete function-body review, including shader-stage specialization/dispatch, module creation, compute/task/mesh limits, ARM data graphs and EXT descriptor-heap mapping/direct-access/struct checks; delegated validators remain separate scopes (partly implemented; compiler-covered cases and unavailable state documented) | New read-only imported shader-header finding adapts byte alignment and magic checks plus the SPIR-V five-word header. It excludes budgets, byte-swapped data, absent stages, unsupported reflection and versions with compilation errors. Actual Godot compilers reject undersized rgba32f imageStore operands and subpass arrays lacking input_attachment_index in both tested versions; existing compile-error reporting covers those. Workgroup/device limits already have rules; subgroup and cooperative-matrix reserve checks need unavailable state. Push ranges, descriptors, YCbCr bindings, native render-pass/depth/stencil/viewport state, transform feedback, enabled long-vector/indexing features, specialization mappings and module-identifier calls cannot be inferred from imported declarations. The SPIRV-Tools validation call is an external delegated scope, not an implemented runtime dependency or a completed review. Follow-up: task/mesh workgroup counts, emitted groups, shared memory and payload limits require stages absent from both public Godot ShaderStage enums. ARM data-graph tensor shapes/formats/bindings/constants and TOSA queue-operation checks need graph pipelines, tensor descriptions and queue-family properties not exposed by RenderingDevice. EXT descriptor-heap mapping offsets/strides, sampler separation, push-data/address alignment, missing mappings and mapped resource restrictions need native mapping/heap state and extension properties. Untyped direct-heap reads also carry extension-specific provisional restrictions, explicitly expected to change for future KHR heaps; no blanket shader rule is inferred. Descriptor-struct member alignment requires unexposed descriptor/tensor heap properties. All bodies in this pinned file have now been read; delegated helpers and SPIRV-Tools validation are not claimed complete. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/core_checks/cc_spirv_cooperative.cpp: Complete handwritten GetComponentType, string_CooperativeMatrixUse, IsSignedIntEnum, ValidateCooperativeMatrix and ValidateCooperativeVector logic, including local helpers, lines 41-1137; delegated SPIRV-Tools constraints are separate (six intrinsic predicates adapted; native feature/property and memory conclusions unavailable) | Complete handwritten cooperative matrix/vector logic read. Six vector predicates now adapt outer-product input type/layout/accumulation interpretation, reduction component types, both training pointer storage classes and FP8 multiplication layouts. Actual Godot 4.7.2 compiler output accepts the invalid cases; 4.4 rejects ordinary cooperative GLSL. Both versions parse saved original bytecode and test imports/error gates without submitting invalid modules. First-entry-block operations, recognized value types/pointer flows/core storage and 32-bit literal layouts/interpretations only; unknown/specialized/helper/later-block cases are omitted independently. Physical-device properties, supported combinations/dimensions, native feature enablement, alignment, memory validity and complete pipeline compatibility remain unknown. Delegated SPIRV-Tools constraints remain separate. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/core_checks/cc_tensor.cpp: Tensor format-feature queries, create/view validation and queue/format/memory requirements (partial core tensor review) | The reviewed checks consume VkTensorARM/VkTensorViewARM handles, ARM tensor tiling/usage, allocation bindings, queue-family indices and capture-replay features. Neither checked Godot RenderingDevice public API exposes tensor resources. Do not map tensor flags onto ordinary ImageTexture or RDTexture settings. Copy/protection/rolling-backing details remain separate scopes. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/drawdispatch/descriptor_validator.cpp: ValidateDescriptorsStatic/ValidateBindingStatic and buffer-descriptor validity dispatch (partial core descriptor review) | Even the static draw-time path requires actual descriptor updates, adjusted allocation counts, native buffer lifetime and memory bindings. Imported shader declarations alone cannot reproduce it. Image/sampler/attachment/tensor branches remain unreviewed. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/gpuav_instrumentation.cpp: Error-record dispatch to registered instrumentation handlers (partial infrastructure review) | Read error-record dispatch and shader lookup; it forwards instrumented GPU records. Remaining orchestration bodies are not claimed reviewed. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/gpuav_shader_instrumentor.cpp: FinishDeviceSetup instrumentation prerequisites and reserved descriptor slot (partial infrastructure review) | These features and the reserved slot support the validation instrumentation itself, not ordinary project requirements. Remaining pipeline/shader rewriting bodies are not claimed reviewed. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/sanitizer.cpp: Divide/modulo zero, ImageGather component, Pow, Atan2, FMin/FMax and cooperative-matrix alignment handlers (partial static adaptation) | Division/remainder/modulo zero and GLSL Pow/Atan2/FMin/FMax now have scalar32 and direct 2-4 component vector adaptations in the first compute entry block. Literal uint32-to-float32 bitcasts are supported. FMin/FMax NaN is portability advice under current Khronos wording, not an invalid-instruction claim. Both Godot compilers reject out-of-range ImageGather components through the existing shader-compile-error rule. General dynamic operands, other widths, later blocks and cooperative-matrix runtime address/alignment checks remain outside observation. Since 1.16, applicable resource/operand checks inspect each public imported shader stage independently, including Godot 4.7 ray stages; workgroup checks remain compute-only. Stage quantities are not summed across unverified pipelines. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/instrumentation/trace_ray.cpp: Ray/hit-object/query values, flags, TLAS state and hit-kind handlers (partial static adaptation) | Direct literal uint32 ray-query flag conflicts and float32 distance constraints in the first compute entry block are adapted. Executed origins/directions, dynamic operands, hit-object/pipeline rays, SBT and TLAS state remain unavailable. No capability-only operand warning is added. Since 1.16, applicable resource/operand checks inspect each public imported shader stage independently, including Godot 4.7 ray stages; workgroup checks remain compute-only. Stage quantities are not summed across unverified pipelines. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/shaders/instrumentation/sanitizer.comp: Divide-zero, gather, Pow, Atan2 and FMin/FMax payload helpers (partial static adaptation) | Payload helpers consume invalidity booleans from the SPIR-V pass. Four bounded static adaptations cover known scalar32/direct vector operands. FMin/FMax deliberately follows current normative NaN portability wording instead of the pinned diagnostic. No GPU execution, instrumentation, pointer/stride checks or general expression evaluation is performed. Since 1.16, applicable resource/operand checks inspect each public imported shader stage independently, including Godot 4.7 ray stages; workgroup checks remain compute-only. Stage quantities are not summed across unverified pipelines. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/shaders/instrumentation/trace_ray.comp: inst_ray_query_comp literal flags and Tmin/Tmax comparisons (partial static adaptation) | Three flag masks and nonnegative/non-NaN/ordered scalar distances are implemented; positive infinity is retained when ordering permits. Origin/direction vectors and dynamic values remain outside this pass. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/buffer_device_address_pass.cpp: RequiresInstrumentation and CreateFunctionCall: physical-pointer access size, live address conversion, range and alignment checks (partial instrumentation review) | The pass converts the executed pointer to uint64 and checks that address against live allocation ranges and required alignment. A declared buffer-reference capability or struct size does not establish an invalid address. Existing device-capability guidance is retained; no static bounds/alignment warning is invented. Access-path construction and the remaining control-flow optimizations are separate review scopes. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/descriptor_buffer_pass.cpp: CreateFunctionCall, RequiresInstrumentation and descriptor-hashing dispatch (partial instrumentation review) | The pass needs native cached descriptor sizes, actual descriptor index/type and descriptor-buffer hashing state. Acceleration structures, sampler/combined-sampler and mutable descriptors are explicitly unsupported by this pass. These are instrumentation coverage limits, not evidence those legal descriptor types are slow or invalid. Godot imported declarations do not expose encoded live descriptor buffers. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/descriptor_class_general_buffer_pass.cpp: RequiresInstrumentation, robustness gates, access-path offsets and instrumentation setup (partial instrumentation review) | General uniform/storage-buffer loads, stores, atomics and cooperative-matrix accesses are filtered by enabled robustness and then checked against actual descriptor binding ranges. Render Lens sees imported declarations but not their live buffer ranges or dispatch. Existing lower-bound declaration/device-limit rules remain distinct from runtime out-of-bounds validation. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/descriptor_class_texel_buffer_pass.cpp: CreateFunctionCall and RequiresInstrumentation: buffer images, fetch/read/write coordinate and binding lookup (partial instrumentation review) | Texel-buffer coordinates and live descriptor ranges are needed. Upstream explicitly limits this path to non-depth, non-arrayed, non-multisampled DimBuffer images and leaves image-offset support incomplete. These checks cannot be generalized into texture-resolution or ordinary storage-image warnings. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/descriptor_heap_pass.cpp: ResourceTypeMatchesBinding, GetMapping, GetMinBufferAlignment and RequiresInstrumentation (partial instrumentation review) | Checks consume VkShaderDescriptorSetAndBindingMappingInfoEXT mappings, live heap selection, embedded-sampler state, physical-device alignment limits and untyped layouts. Shader declarations alone do not supply those values. Native mapping masks and instrumentation exclusions must not become generic Godot resource warnings. Remaining offset/sampler call-generation logic is a separate review scope. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/descriptor_indexing_oob_pass.cpp: RequiresInstrumentation and image/sampler call arguments: descriptor indices, binding-layout counts and per-block deduplication (partial instrumentation review) | Checks need the pipeline binding-layout lookup table, executed image/sampler descriptor indices and bindless state. Shader declaration lengths alone do not establish the bound allocation count or an executed invalid access. Ordinary non-array descriptors without bindless state are explicitly skipped upstream. No generic material-count adaptation is appropriate. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/sanitizer_pass.cpp: Division/modulo dispatch, ImageGather compiler rejection, constant-zero recognition and extended-math component predicates (partial static adaptation) | Reviewed RequiresInstrumentation, IsConstantZero and PowCheck/Atan2Check/FminmaxCheck. Seven divide/remainder/modulo opcodes exclude OpFDiv. Direct scalar32/vector components and literal bitcasts are adapted; unknown components are not guessed. Only the exact GLSL.std.450 import is accepted. Compiler-folded operations can leave no instruction to inspect. ImageGather component errors are already rejected by both Godot compilers. Runtime and cooperative-matrix instrumentation remains unadapted. Since 1.16, applicable resource/operand checks inspect each public imported shader stage independently, including Godot 4.7 ray stages; workgroup checks remain compute-only. Stage quantities are not summed across unverified pipelines. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/shared_memory_data_race_pass.cpp: RequiresInstrumentation dispatch, Workgroup barrier filtering, access-chain offsets and GetWorkgroupSize (partial instrumentation review) | Race checks need inter-invocation shadow state and actual memory accesses; a shared declaration, atomic instruction or barrier alone does not prove a race. The reviewed path resets shadow state for Workgroup AcquireRelease control barriers. Its instrumentation assumptions are not a blanket requirement that every compute shader contain such a barrier. Existing fixed workgroup/shared-memory limit checks are separate. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/spec_constant.cpp: SetSpecConstantValue and component handling in ConstantFold (partial specialization review) | Upstream freezes defaults only after consulting the actual VkSpecializationInfo and matching map entries, then folds expressions using those resolved values. Render Lens does not observe pipeline-creation specialization data. Treating imported defaults as effective values would create false workgroup/resource/operand findings, so unresolved specialization remains excluded. General arithmetic/vector folding is not replicated. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/trace_ray_pass.cpp: GetRayQueryInitializeValidationFunctionCallInstructions operand wiring (partial instrumentation review) | Verified flag/origin/Tmin/direction/Tmax operand positions against OpRayQueryInitializeKHR and Godot-bundled SPIR-V enums. Other ray/hit-object pass transformations need further review. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/state_tracker/shader_module.cpp: GetLocationsConsumedByType, GetComponentsConsumedByType, StageInterfaceVariable::GetInterfaceSlots and entry-point slot-map population; IsArrayInterface and FindBaseType; focused invocation-repack switch fragment at lines 1093-1103 only (partial interface metadata review) | Read numeric type/width matching, component spans, matrix-column gaps, array location strides and explicit member/variable decorations. The plugin implements bounded reflection and skips unknown/grouped layouts; it is not a complete SPIR-V validator. Other module parsing, reachability, built-in use and feature analysis remain separate scopes. Fragment PerVertexKHR arrays discard exactly their outer wrapper for matching; tested on both Godot compilers. Nested structure layout follows the normative location-assignment rules and is compiler-tested rather than inheriting the upstream nested-structure workaround. The focused switch sets the repack flag for TraceRayKHR, TraceRayMotionNV, ReportIntersectionKHR and ExecuteCallableKHR; this does not complete unrelated module instruction cases or adaptation of the stateless control-flow conflict. The focused invocation-repack switch now supports a tested intrinsic subgroup-mode conflict rule. Unrelated instruction tracking remains outside the recorded scope. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/state_tracker/shader_module.h: InterfaceSlot definition only (partial interface metadata review) | Location/component mapping and numeric-kind/bit-width fields support the new interface finding. This header read does not claim full shader state analysis or native Vulkan feature coverage. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/stateless/sl_pipeline.cpp: Pipeline-layout set count and push-constant ranges (partially adapted) | Godot reflects sets through highest index + 1 and push constants into a zero-offset range. Other pipeline creation parameters require separate review. Since 1.16, applicable resource/operand checks inspect each public imported shader stage independently, including Godot 4.7 ray stages; workgroup checks remain compute-only. Stage quantities are not summed across unverified pipelines. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/stateless/sl_spirv.cpp: Complete handwritten bodies and local helpers, lines 18-1781, including all 27 dispatch targets/helpers; delegated SPIRV-Tools and generated capability tables are separate scopes (handwritten logic review complete; four intrinsic predicates adapted; unexposed features/properties excluded) | Existing eight-bit shader IO plus GraphARM in shader modules, tensor rank/shape declaration forms and subgroup-uniform-control-flow with invocation repacking are adapted. Both compilers accept GraphARM and the mode/intrinsic ReportIntersection combination; Godot 4.7 compiles a valid ranked tensor module and both versions test declaration mutations in saved bytecode. All checks require Vulkan and error-free imported versions; no invalid shader/pipeline is created. Remaining subgroup/tessellation/mesh/geometry/transform-feedback/tile/tensor/float-control/texel-offset/conservative-rasterization/wait-queue checks require unexposed enabled features, exact physical-device properties or unsupported Godot stage/pipeline state; no native limits or unsupported-feature conclusions are guessed. This completes only this handwritten source file, not delegated or other Vulkan validation. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/sync/sync_access_state.cpp: DetectHazard and DetectAsyncHazard (partial synchronization review) | Read-after-write, write-after-read/write and racing accesses compare recorded reads/writes, stage/access scopes, queue IDs and usage tags. Presence of a shared resource or missing script barrier is insufficient. Other state-update/attachment/present paths are not claimed reviewed. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/sync/sync_barrier.cpp: BarrierSet buffer/image memory-barrier normalization (partial synchronization review) | Uses actual VkBuffer/VkImage handles, offsets/sizes/subresource ranges, stage/access masks and old/new layouts, including depth-slice normalization. These native barriers cannot be reconstructed from project settings. Remaining barrier application paths need review. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/sync/sync_hazard_detection.cpp: AccessContext buffer/image DetectHazard entry points (partial synchronization review) | Hazards operate on native buffer address ranges or image subresources with current usage and prior access contexts. Scene order or resource sharing does not reveal these ranges or command history; attachment and submit paths need further review. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/vulkan/generated/spirv_validation_helper.cpp: Complete CompatibleSpirvImageFormat and ValidateShaderCapabilitiesAndExtensions bodies; all 41 image mappings; seven forbidden SPIR-V 1.0 capability exclusions and PhysicalStorageBufferAddresses/RayQueryKHR/RayTracingKHR feature-table entries only; remaining generated tables/helpers pending (mapping and selected declaration/feature predicates adapted; other tables/helpers pending) | The complete validator uses alternative version/feature/extension/property requirements and a separate portability interpolation feature gate. Stable seven-capability exclusions and the mandatory SPV_ extension namespace are observable in compiled imports and pass native compiler/import tests on both versions; unrelated unknown/future capabilities or SPV_ extensions are not rejected. Declaration findings require a version without stored base or stage errors and the actual Vulkan driver. Only public Godot support getters are used for physical buffer addresses, ray queries and ray-tracing pipelines; missing queries are unknown. Ray-tracing support is the engine compound acceleration-structure/pipeline contract, not a raw Vulkan feature query. Full enabled-feature/property/extension tables and other generated helpers are not counted reviewed. Prior 41-format mappings retain their current native support tests and tiling exclusions. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |

## Removed by product policy

| Candidate | Reason | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Validation-layer log rules (VUID, SYNC-HAZARD, D3D12 message IDs) and api_validation_capture | Removed in 1.3+: Render Lens no longer depends on the Vulkan SDK, VkConfig, validation layers or child validation launches. | CHANGELOG.md; README.md |
| layers/gpuav/descriptor_validation/gpuav_descriptor_set.cpp: Descriptor binding layout and resource-encoding snapshots, including push updates (validation infrastructure) | Encodes actual native descriptor classes, resource IDs and byte/texel ranges; imported declarations cannot supply these runtime bindings or resource lifetimes. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/descriptor_validation/gpuav_descriptor_validation.cpp: UpdateBoundDescriptors and UpdateBoundDescriptorHeap (validation infrastructure) | Routes current command-buffer descriptor sets and heap snapshots to registered validators; no independent project performance predicate. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/spirv/post_process_descriptor_indexing_pass.cpp: CreateFunctionCall, RequiresInstrumentation and Instrument (validation infrastructure) | This pass records actually used descriptor set/binding/index/variable combinations for later validation against live native binding state. It does not make unused shader declarations invalid. Its block-level deduplication and instrumentation limits are validator implementation details, not Godot game performance rules. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
| layers/gpuav/validation_cmd/gpuav_validation_cmd_common.cpp: Validation resource and pipeline binding helpers (validation infrastructure) | GPU validation infrastructure, not a separate project configuration predicate. | docs/VULKAN_COVERAGE.json |
